  filter( !is.na(v1) )

df <- data.frame(x = 1:10,
                 y = c(2,4,6,8,10,
                       1,3,5,7,9))
df <- df |> 
  mutate(
    x_lag = lag(x),  # previous value
    x_lead = lead(x),  # next value
    y_cumsum = cumsum(y),    # running sum
    y_cummean = cummean(y),   # running average
    y_cummin = cummin(y),    # running minimum
    y_cummax = cummax(y)    # running maximum
  )
rank_me <- data.frame(x = c(10, 5, 1, 5, 5, NA))

rank_me |>
  mutate(
    id = row_number(),             # row id (1, 2, 3, ...)
    x_row_number = row_number(x),  # tie-breaking rank (arbitrary but stable)
    x_min_rank   = min_rank(x),    # ranks with gaps when ties occur
    x_dense_rank = dense_rank(x),  # ranks without gaps when ties occur
    x_percent    = percent_rank(x),# 0 to 1 (excluding 1 unless max)
    x_cume       = cume_dist(x)    # 0 to 1 (including 1 for max)
  )
   x id x_row_number x_min_rank x_dense_rank x_percent x_cume

3  1  3            1          1            1      0.00    0.2

5  5  5            4          2            2      0.25    0.8

if_else() vs ifelse()

# if_else()
flight_season <- flights |>
  mutate(
    summer_month = 
      if_else(month %in% c(6, 7, 8), 
              TRUE, FALSE)
  )
flight_season <- flights |>
  mutate(
    summer_month = 
      ifelse(month %in% c(6, 7, 8), 
             TRUE, 0)  # coerces to 
  )                    #    numeric

case_when()

scores <- tibble(score = c(95, 82, 74, 61, NA))
scores |>
  mutate(
    grade = case_when(
      is.na(score)      ~ "missing",
      score >= 90       ~ "A",
      score >= 80       ~ "B",
      score >= 70       ~ "C",
      score >= 60       ~ "D",
      TRUE              ~ "F"
    )
  )
# A tibble: 5 × 2
  score grade  
  <dbl> <chr>  
1    95 A      
2    82 B      
3    74 C

  relocate(tailnum)
q7 <- flights |> 
  mutate(is_delayed = ifelse(arr_delay >= 30, T, F)) |> 
  count(hour, is_delayed) |> 
  group_by(hour) |> 
  mutate(prop = 100 * n / sum(n)) |> 
  filter(is_delayed) |> 
  arrange(prop)
q9 <- flights |>
  count(dest, carrier) |>
  group_by(dest) |> 
  filter( n() >= 2) |> 
  group_by(carrier) |> 
  summarize(tot_flights = sum(n)) |> 
  arrange(-tot_flights)
